<big>**Welcome to the main SOMA Scripting Function Reference Api.**  

This page lists important and useful functions on variety of topics.</big>

---

You can find more useful functions in the **helper files** section of the main scripting page. **No additional script files are needed to be included in order to call these functions. They can be used as-is.**  

This page is split into main scripting topics, each includes its own sub-topics as seen in the table below.

:::tip[Tip]
Each section has a "See More" option, should you want to get more information about function parameters and descriptions.
:::

## General
*[Constants](/api/wiki-index/#constants) - Constant values which can be used everywhere.
*[Map](/api/wiki-index/#map) - General Map functions (Adding timers, Loading maps, etc)
*[Material](/api/wiki-index/#material) - Materials management functions.

## Effects
*[Light](/api/wiki-index/#light) - Functions for controlling light (PointLight, SpotLight, BoxLight).
*[ParticleSystem](/api/wiki-index/#particlesystem) - Functions for creating and controlling particles.
*[Billboard](/api/wiki-index/#billboard) - Functions for controlling billboards.
*[FogArea](/api/wiki-index/#fogarea) - Functions for controlling Fog Areas.
*[LensFlare](/api/wiki-index/#lensflare) - Functions for controlling Lens Flares.
*[IrradianceSet](/api/wiki-index/#irradianceset) - Functions for controlling SH Probes

## Entities
*[Entity](/api/wiki-index/#entity) - General Entity functions (Activate/Deactivate, Create/Destroy, etc). **Applies to all entity types.**
*[Tool](/api/wiki-index/#tool)
*[Prop](/api/wiki-index/#prop)
*[Lamp](/api/wiki-index/#lamp)
*[SlideDoor](/api/wiki-index/#slidedoor)
*[SwingDoor](/api/wiki-index/#swingdoor)
*[LevelDoor](/api/wiki-index/#leveldoor)
*[PhysicsSlideDoor](/api/wiki-index/#physicsslidedoor)
*[MoveObject](/api/wiki-index/#moveobject)
*[MovingButton](/api/wiki-index/#movingbutton)
*[Button](/api/wiki-index/#button)
*[Lever](/api/wiki-index/#lever)
*[Wheel](/api/wiki-index/#wheel)
*[Grab](/api/wiki-index/#grab)
*[Slide](/api/wiki-index/#slide)
*[Readable](/api/wiki-index/#readable) - Readable entities functions (Notes, inspectable objects, etc)
*[Terminal](/api/wiki-index/#terminal) - Terminal entities functions (GUI).
*[EnergySource](/api/wiki-index/#energysource) - WAU Flowers entities functions.
*[Meter](/api/wiki-index/#meter)

## Engine
*[cLux](/api/wiki-index/#clux) - General development and debug functions. 
*[cScript](/api/wiki-index/#cscript) - [Global Variables](/scripting/local-and-global-variables/) functions.
*[cMath](/api/wiki-index/#cmath) - Various basic and advanced math operations.
*[cGui](/api/wiki-index/#cgui) - Core GUI functions.
*[cInput](/api/wiki-index/#cinput) - Input detection and keybinding functions.
*[cString](/api/wiki-index/#cstring) - Utility functions for [tString](/api/classes/tstring/).
*[cScene](/api/wiki-index/#cscene) - Functions related to basic map components. This is mostly regarding object conversion and viewport manipulation. 
*[cSound](/api/wiki-index/#csound) - Utility functions for sound.
*[cPhysics](/api/wiki-index/#cphysics) - Utility functions for physics and physics bodies.
*[cAI](/api/wiki-index/#cai) - Event database functions for AI components.
*[cGenerate](/api/wiki-index/#cgenerate) - Effect generation functions represented by numeric values.
*[cGraphics](/api/wiki-index/#cgraphics) - Low-level engine functions for managing various graphics operations.
*[cResources](/api/wiki-index/#cresources) - Low-level functions for managing engine resources and files. 
*[cEngine](/api/wiki-index/#cengine) - Low-level engine functions for monitoring and controlling core data.
*[cSystem](/api/wiki-index/#csystem) - Low-level OS functions, including file and [thread](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Thread_(computing)) management. **Use with caution!** 
*[Logging](/api/wiki-index/#logging) - Debug logging functions. Most of them do not work.
*[Hashing](/api/wiki-index/#hashing) - Basic hashing functions (64 and 32 bits).

## Physics
*[Body](/api/wiki-index/#body) - Physics Body functions.
*[Joint](/api/wiki-index/#joint) - Physics Joints functions.

## Miscellaneous
*[String](/api/wiki-index/#string) - Old Api functions for string. New one is [cString](/api/wiki-index/#cstring).
*[Math](/api/wiki-index/#math) - Old Api functions for math. New one is [cMath](/api/wiki-index/#cmath).
